Part 1 - Câu 1<br><br><img src="part1_q01.png"><br>[sound:part1_q01.mp3]<br><br><div class="mc-container" data-ans="A"><button class="mc-btn" onclick="checkMC(this, 'A')">(A) She’s eating in a picnic area.</button><button class="mc-btn" onclick="checkMC(this, 'B')">(B) She’s waiting in line at a food truck.</button><button class="mc-btn" onclick="checkMC(this, 'C')">(C) She’s wiping off a bench.</button><button class="mc-btn" onclick="checkMC(this, 'D')">(D) She’s throwing away a plate.</button></div><script>function checkMC(btn, choice){var parent = btn.parentElement; if(parent.dataset.answered) return; parent.dataset.answered = "true"; var correct = parent.dataset.ans; var btns = parent.getElementsByTagName("button"); for(var i=0; i<btns.length; i++){ if(btns[i].textContent.trim().startsWith("(" + correct + ")")){ btns[i].style.background = "#28a745"; btns[i].style.color = "#fff"; btns[i].style.borderColor = "#28a745"; } } if(choice !== correct){ btn.style.background = "#dc3545"; btn.style.color = "#fff"; btn.style.borderColor = "#dc3545"; }}</script>	<b>Đáp án đúng: (A)</b><br><br><b>Giải thích:</b> Người phụ nữ đang ngồi ăn ở bàn dã ngoại ngoài trời.

Part 1 - Câu 1<br><br><img src="test2_part1_q01.png"><br>[sound:test2_part1_q01.mp3]<br><br><div class="mc-container" data-ans="A"><button class="mc-btn" onclick="checkMC(this, 'A')">(A) She’s inserting a cord into an outlet.</button><button class="mc-btn" onclick="checkMC(this, 'B')">(B) She’s pressing a button on a machine.</button><button class="mc-btn" onclick="checkMC(this, 'C')">(C) She’s gripping the handle of a drawer.</button><button class="mc-btn" onclick="checkMC(this, 'D')">(D) She’s tacking a notice onto the wall.</button></div><script>function checkMC(btn, choice){var parent = btn.parentElement; if(parent.dataset.answered) return; parent.dataset.answered = "true"; var correct = parent.dataset.ans; var btns = parent.getElementsByTagName("button"); for(var i=0; i<btns.length; i++){ if(btns[i].textContent.trim().startsWith("(" + correct + ")")){ btns[i].style.background = "#28a745"; btns[i].style.color = "#fff"; btns[i].style.borderColor = "#28a745"; } } if(choice !== correct){ btn.style.background = "#dc3545"; btn.style.color = "#fff"; btn.style.borderColor = "#dc3545"; }}</script>	<b>Đáp án đúng: (A) She’s inserting a cord into an outlet.</b><br><br><b>Giải thích:</b> Người phụ nữ đang cắm dây điện vào ổ cắm trên tường.

Part 3 - Câu 70<br><br>[sound:test4_part3_c68_70.mp3]<br><b>Question 70: According to the man, why has some work been delayed?</b><br><br><div class="mc-container" data-ans="B"><button class="mc-btn" onclick="checkMC(this, 'A')">(A) Funding was cut.</button><button class="mc-btn" onclick="checkMC(this, 'B')">(B) Thunderstorms occurred frequently.</button><button class="mc-btn" onclick="checkMC(this, 'C')">(C) Key equipment malfunctioned.</button><button class="mc-btn" onclick="checkMC(this, 'D')">(D) Permits took long to approve.</button></div><script>function checkMC(btn, choice){var parent = btn.parentElement; if(parent.dataset.answered) return; parent.dataset.answered = "true"; var correct = parent.dataset.ans; var btns = parent.getElementsByTagName("button"); for(var i=0; i<btns.length; i++){ if(btns[i].textContent.trim().startsWith("(" + correct + ")")){ btns[i].style.background = "#28a745"; btns[i].style.color = "#fff"; btns[i].style.borderColor = "#28a745"; } } if(choice !== correct){ btn.style.background = "#dc3545"; btn.style.color = "#fff"; btn.style.borderColor = "#dc3545"; }}</script>	<b>Đáp án đúng: (B) Thunderstorms occurred frequently.</b><br><br><i>Transcript:</i> Man: "We'd actually hoped to be further along, but there've been lots of thunderstorms lately, which have slowed things down."<br><br><b>Dịch nghĩa:</b> Người đàn ông giải thích tiến độ bị chậm lại do dạo gần đây thường xuyên có dông bão.
